- **The 425 case ids (V21-B1, resolved by V22 item 1; V23 item 2 preserved; V24 items 2–5 scope the intern and collision statements; V25 item 1 preserves the identical case ids on the F46/B2″ synthetic expectation variant).** The case ids are the real generated ids from the live expectation and the real generator `turnvector_benchmark/lane_contract.py` (`expand_case_plan`): `case_id = f"{lane.lane_id}.{matrix.matrix_id}.{ordinal:04d}"` with the ordinal incrementing once per Cartesian-product combination (`product(*values)` in dimension order then dimension-value order) and continuing across matrices within a lane. The exact length distribution over the 425 generated ids, independently recomputed this revision from the live bytes, is `{34:12, 35:6, 37:36, 41:67, 42:12, 43:20, 45:16, 47:15, 48:119, 50:6, 53:24, 54:12, 55:80}` with the true minimum 34 (`mlx-native-correctness.decode.0001` = 22+1+6+1+4), 54 ids shorter than 38 bytes, 169 shorter than 47 bytes, and maximum 55; **the first id in the frozen traversal order is `core-event-replay.effect-result-replay.0001` (43 bytes) — the C4 collision target of V24 item 4**. The exact case-id intern charge **on the frozen collision-free basis (V24 item 2)** is `Σ(16 + len(c)) = 26,618` (per-lane case-id length multisets: lane 0 `{43:20}`, lane 1 `{41:3}`, lane 2 `{41:40}`, lane 3 `{47:15}`, lane 4 `{34:12,35:6}`, lane 5 `{41:24,42:12}`, lane 6 `{55:80}`, lane 7 `{37:36}`, lane 8 `{53:24,54:12}`, lane 9 `{45:16,50:6}`, lane 10 `{48:24}`, lane 11 `{48:95}`; per-lane length sums `[860,123,1640,705,618,1488,4400,1332,1920,1020,1152,4560]` totaling 19,818). **Every false premise that `len(c) >= 38` under the real matrix names, that every case id is charged at a uniform 47 bytes, or that the intern lower bound is 195,255 is deleted; no length floor is inferred from names (V22 item 1). Under the general contract (V23 item 2 preserved; V24 item 5) the substep makes 425 visits with zero to 425 intern allocations: a hit visit charges 0 new arena bytes and creates no entry, and only miss visits contribute `16 + len(c)` each; the exact 26,618 charge and the "all 425 visits are misses" statements apply only to the frozen collision-free basis/fixtures of V24 item 2 — including the F46/B2″ synthetic expectation variant of V25 item 1, whose 425 case ids are identical to the live basis — and the live basis of V24 item 3.** **Live-basis prior-intern enumeration (V24 item 3, re-enumerated this revision read-only; the identifier-typed value convention is the exact enumeration rule of V25 item 2: every JSON string value matching the stable identifier grammar `^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9._-]*$`, excluding the lowercase 64-hex class; the 64-hex exclusion is justified by structural disjointness — every generated case id contains literal dots and is 34–55 bytes, while every lowercase 64-hex string contains no dot and is exactly 64 bytes — not by claiming the enumerated set is literally a superset of every schema-valid identifier value and not by claiming every 64-hex string is digest-typed):**

The 59 occurrences sum as 7 + 17 + 7 + 7 + 16 + 1 + 1 + 1 + 1 + 1 = 59; the 25 distinct sum as 7 + 7 + 2 + 2 + 2 + 1 + 1 + 1 + 1 + 1 = 25.**

The PR 5 catalog fixture `tests/fixtures/obligation-catalog/synthetic-catalog-v1.jsonl` (sha256 `20db49537d8032f321bae99430458d1ad5b1a9ccb2295e45b63a8d2c97269fac`, 48 records = header + 47 body obligation records) — **689 grammar-matching occurrences, 199 distinct**, of which 146 are 64-hex digest occurrences (section/source-file/expectation digest values); **non-hex 543 occurrences, 138 distinct**, comprising the header id `synthetic-obligation-catalog-v1` and the six other header grammar values (`kind` `catalog`, `profile_id` `turnvector-implementation-v2`, `schema_version` `turnvector.benchmark.obligation-catalog.v1`, `compile_custody_lineage_id` `tvb-qualification-d0-catalog-v1`, `custody_domain_id` `synthetic-custody-domain-v1`, `lineage_id` `synthetic-obligation-catalog-lineage-v1`), the 47 body obligation ids `obligation-0001`…`obligation-0047`, the real `lane_id`/`behavior_case_id` values (bound to the real expectation), the synthetic `seam_id`/`observable_seam`/`claim_class` values, the `kind`/`evidence_grade` consts, and the readiness/`module_ids`/`blocker_ids` values — enumerated by the same values-only JSON string rule.
  **Union across the three materialized artifacts: 568 distinct non-hex grammar-matching strings. Verified this revision: the intersection of the union — and of each input's own set — with the 425 generated case-id set is empty (zero collisions). No complete all-five-input live union is claimed:** PR 6 does not materialize an exact live `authority/traceability-v1.json` (the ledger is authored by the catalog-content gate / PR 8; PR 6 ships only its schema and private parser with synthetic fixtures — re-observed: `authority/` contains only `source-reconciliation-v1.json`) and does not materialize an exact live AuthoritySnapshot (PR 7 produces it), so the existing verified observation is scoped to the currently materialized expectation/source-reconciliation/PR 5 catalog artifacts, and generated-fixture completeness is guaranteed by the frozen complete-set assertion of V24 item 2 under the exact enumeration rule of V25 item 2. **The false expectation-identifier count carried by V23 is deleted** (no natural enumeration of the expectation matches it: the canonical identifier domains give 292 occurrences / 241 distinct, and the conservative superset gives 819 occurrences / 428 distinct).
- `m_l` — explicit obligation-gate pair count in lane l; SPECIFIED by the later ledger. `M=sum_l m_l`, with `max(O_l,G_l)<=m_l<=O_l*G_l`, so `59<=M<=225`. **The F46/B2″ family freezes the complete-bipartite assignment `m_l = O_l·G_l` = `[12,9,12,20,16,16,28,28,28,16,20,20]` (M = 225), giving H = 8,899 and R_HE = 41,883 (V25 item 1, §15).**
- `H = Σ_l C_l·m_l` — enforcement-path count; `R_HE = Σ_l C_l·m_l·D_l`; path-subgraph metrics `Q_path = 4H + R_DE + R_HE + R_GNG + R_GNP + R_LC + R_LP = 4H + R_HE + 211` and `R_path = 5H + 2(R_DE + R_HE + R_CH + R_GH + R_HNJ + R_GNG + R_GNP + R_LC + R_LP) = 11H + 2R_HE + 422` (conventions: each enforcement-path record contributes 5 endpoint references — its five foreign-key fields; `path_id` is the record's own identity and is not an endpoint reference; each relation record contributes 2 endpoint references — the accepted parent convention `2M + 208`, independent of serialized field count); full totals `Q_total = Q_path + M + 104 = 4H + R_HE + M + 315` and `R_total = R_path + 2M + 208 = 11H + 2R_HE + 2M + 630`; `entity_count = H + 852`; `relation_only = 3H + R_HE + M + 315` — counts, DERIVED.
- `K_exec = C + R_HE + 3H + 4G + R_LC + R_LP`; `K_run = K_exec + 2t + 6` — counts, DERIVED; support relations generate no keys, so K formulas are unchanged.
- `U_key = Σ_{k∈names} (len(k_bytes)+1)` — total bytes of the complete sorted canonical expected-key stream, each ASCII key name followed by LF — bytes, DERIVED; it is exactly the `expected_keys.sha256` preimage length.
- `U_digest` — the exact total bytes of all 27 canonical entity/relation digest-stream preimages (§2.4), each preimage being the byte concatenation of one compact recursively lexical-key JSON object plus one LF per record in stream order — bytes, DERIVED (see the exact decomposition below).
- `A`, `S`, `B`, `X`, `P` — bytes: authority source sizes, section bytes with catalog multiplicity, the five serialized inputs, exact peak logical arena charge, and final output. Caller observations supply atomic lengths; sums are DERIVED and checked. `W_B = 2,712` — the frozen Phase B scratch pre-reservation, bytes, FROZEN CONSTANT, part of X (V17-B1 as applied by V18; V19 item 1 re-verifies; V20 item 2 re-verifies; V21 item 4 freezes the one-`bytearray` implementation). **Each S8 SHA-256 state is 256 logical bytes, one at a time: retained through `reserve(256)` at `used = 0` and released before the next state and before the W_B pre-reservation, so the logical x_max guard cannot fire at S8 (`0 + 256 = 256 <= 33,554,432`, proved — V19 item 1). The W_B reservation is `0 + 2,712 = 2,712 <= 33,554,432` at `used = 0` (proved), so its logical 46 cannot fire either (V20 item 2, V21 item 2). The `h_max` logical `reserve(65,536)` fires only if `used + 65,536 > x_max` at its K1 (Phase A) or K4 (Phase D) reservation; the Phase D reservation reuses the single Phase A physical bytearray — no physical allocation (V22 item 2). The case-id intern miss reservation is `used + (16 + len(c)) <= x_max`; a hit visit performs no reservation (V23 item 2, V24 items 2–5).**
- `M_aux = h_max + l_max + x_max + q_max = 65,536 + 4,194,304 + 33,554,432 + 65,536 = 37,879,808` bytes — compiler-controlled auxiliary bound, ANALYTIC (logical, not RSS); unchanged by the S8 states, the W_B pre-reservation, and the single physical `h_max` allocation with its two logical reservations (all lie inside the x_max envelope; the host bytes of the one physical `h_max` buffer are exactly the analytic `h_max` constant and no second buffer exists — V22 item 2).

### Equations and substitutions
```
H_min = Σ_l C_l·max(O_l,G_l) = 2,287        H_max = Σ_l C_l·O_l·G_l = 8,899
R_HE_min = Σ_l C_l·max(O_l,G_l)·D_l = 10,693  R_HE_max = Σ_l C_l·O_l·G_l·D_l = 41,883

R_path = 11H + 2R_HE + 422 :   46,965 ≤ R_path ≤ 182,077

18,254 ≤ K_exec ≤ 69,280    18,262 ≤ K_run ≤ 69,302 (1 ≤ t ≤ 8)

A ≤ 67,108,864   S ≤ 33,554,432   B ≤ 16,777,216   X ≤ 33,554,432   P ≤ 33,554,432
U_key ≤ P ≤ 33,554,432
W_B = 2,712 (part of X; every Phase-B-admitted input satisfies parse-retained ≤ x_max − W_B = 33,551,720)

```

**V28 contract arithmetic audit (mandatory; independently re-run read-only from the current bytes; V26's self-reported recomputation is historical only and is not accepted gate evidence; every lane substitution, count, equation, bound, sensitivity ratio, arena maximum, and complexity statement below was recomputed independently for this revision from the live expectation bytes and the frozen stream projections; every displayed arithmetic equality was machine-checked against the live bytes; the reconciliation enumeration was re-run from the current `authority/source-reconciliation-v1.json` bytes and the corrected occurrence decomposition `7 + 17 + 7 + 7 + 16 + 1 + 1 + 1 + 1 + 1 = 59` and the distinct decomposition `7 + 7 + 2 + 2 + 2 + 1 + 1 + 1 + 1 + 1 = 25` were reproduced exactly (V26 item 1); the F46/B2″ termination chain `2,784 + 4,254·8,899 + 24·41,883 = 38,864,322 > 33,554,432` is collision-independent — it deliberately assumes zero parse-retained and zero generated-case-id intern charge and is therefore valid for every possible hit/miss sequence; no parse-retained subtotal and no derived lower bound is displayed anywhere in this design — V21-B1 resolved by V22 item 1, V23 item 1 superseded by V24 item 1, preserved by V25 item 1; the worked-example charge was re-verified against the live fixture bytes — V18-B3 resolved, V19 item 3 re-verified, V20 re-verified, V21 re-verified, V22 re-verified, V23 re-verified, V24 re-verified, V25 re-verified, V26 re-verifies).** The recomputation confirms: `M_min = Σ max(O_l,G_l) = 59`; `M_max = Σ O_l·G_l = 225`; `H_min = Σ C_l·max(O_l,G_l) = 2,287`; `H_max = Σ C_l·O_l·G_l = 8,899`; `R_HE_min = 10,693`; `R_HE_max = 41,883`; `Q_path` endpoints `4·2,287+10,693+211 = 20,052` and `4·8,899+41,883+211 = 77,690`; `K_run = K_exec + 2t + 6` with endpoints `18,262` (t=1) and `69,302` (t=8); sensitivity ratios `x_max/R_total_max = 33,554,432/182,735 = 183.623…` and `p_max/K_run_max = 33,554,432/69,302 = 484.177…`; `M_aux = 37,879,808`; the `U_digest` closed form `35,158 + 177H + 47M + 39R_HE + V_streams` (fixed constants re-summed to 35,158; `N_val = 2,161 + 12H + 3M + 2R_HE`; `path_id` occurrences `4H + R_HE`; the corrected finite bound `U_digest <= 35,158 + 177H + 47M + 39R_HE + (2,161 + 8H + 3M + R_HE)·L_id_max + (4H + R_HE)·(5·L_id_max + 4)`, which at the upper count endpoint evaluates to `U_digest <= 3,564,209 + 503,306·L_id_max`); the exact case-id basis (length distribution, per-lane multisets, intern charge 26,618 on the frozen collision-free basis, first id `core-event-replay.effect-result-replay.0001` at 43 bytes); the live-basis prior-intern enumeration of V24 item 3 under the exact enumeration rule of V25 item 2 (expectation 819/428, reconciliation 59/25 non-hex with the exact class definition of V25 item 3 and the occurrence decomposition of V26 item 1, catalog 543/138 non-hex, three-input union 568, zero intersection with the 425 case ids; no false expectation-identifier count; uppercase `TurnVector` not counted); the `x_max − W_B` invariant and every W_B/S8/h_max guard; and every K0–K4 counter formula and golden substitution (including the K3 `320 + M`/`643 + 2M` golden with exact summands). **The F46/B2″ fixture identities are re-verified: the 1,024-byte `fixture-gate-<NNNN>` forms (13 + 4 + 1007 = 1,024 bytes), the per-lane ordinal slices of §15, the complete-bipartite M = 225 / H = 8,899 / R_HE = 41,883, and the 4,134-byte minimum `len(h)` (`4·1,024 + 34 + 4`), all preserved from V25–V27 and re-audited for V28.**

The three-field config record and fixed-size digest representation remain unchanged; provenance-only `argv` retains the exact eight-key command record; an identity remains nine keys; `BoundBytesRef.nbytes` is unchanged; and recursive lexical output-key ordering is a member permutation. Therefore the 27-stream constants, `U_digest`, `U_key`, `P_nonkey`, `P`, all K0–K4 counters, F46/B2″/C4, 425 visits, the 48-stage ordinal, the 68-field CompileLimits vector, and every displayed formula/bound remain numerically unchanged.

  → complete: 398 + 2M + 116 + 116 + 11H + 2R_HE = 630 + 2M + 11H + 2R_HE = R_total

### K_exec rederivation from all eleven exact execution families (V8 disposition item 8)

The accepted implementation bundle v9 freezes the eleven execution families (§7.3). Their key counts are: `case/c` → `|C| = 425`; `lane-context/l/a` → `R_LC = 21`; `evidence/h/e` → `R_HE`; `bundle/h` → `H`; `judge/h` → `H`; `gate-input/g` → `G = 58`; `gate-result/g` → `G = 58`; `judge-negative/h/n` → `H`; `gate-negative/g/n` → `G = 58`; `plumbing-negative/g/n` → `G = 58`; `lane-output/l/a` → `R_LP = 22`. Therefore `K_exec = 425 + R_LC + R_HE + 3H + 4·58 + R_LP = C + R_HE + 3H + 4G + R_LC + R_LP`, giving `18,254 ≤ K_exec ≤ 69,280` and `K_run = K_exec + 2t + 6` with `18,262 ≤ K_run ≤ 69,302`. **Reconciliation with parent v7:** the accepted parent bundle v7's `X_p` expression was `|C_p|+|R_HE|+2|H_OCJDG|+2|G_p|+|R_HNJ|+|R_GNG|+|R_GNP| = C+R_HE+3H+4G`, which omitted the lane-context and lane-output families — exactly `R_LC + R_LP = 21 + 22 = 43` keys. Implementation bundle v9 is the controlling accepted successor for v2 key composition; V25 states that the 43 added context/output keys reconcile, rather than silently contradict, parent v7: v7's `X_p` predates the frozen artifact partition's explicit 21 pre-dispatch lane-context memberships and 22 post-gate lane-output memberships (§12.2), which the later accepted bundle v9 freezes as the `lane-context/l/a` and `lane-output/l/a` execution families. V26 preserves `K_run = K_exec + 2t + 6` and rederives it in this gate from all eleven exact execution families. The `K_exec` value carried by V6–V25 (`K_exec = 700 + R_HE + 3H` in `[18,254, 69,280]`) recomputes exactly and is unchanged.

### Exact `U_digest` accounting (V7 disposition item 7 and V8 disposition item 6)

Every digest-stream record is one compact lexical-key JSON object plus one LF. For a stream `s` whose frozen projection has `f_s` fields with key-name byte lengths `len(key_f)` and per-record value byte lengths `len(value_f(r))`, the per-record bytes including the final LF are exactly

```
‖record_s(r)‖ = 6·f_s + 2 + Σ_f len(key_f) + Σ_f len(value_f(r))
```

(the `{`, `}`, `f_s` quoted keys, `f_s` colons, `f_s` quoted string values, and `f_s−1` commas, plus the LF). Therefore

```
U_digest = Σ_s Σ_{r∈s} ‖record_s(r)‖
         = Σ_s [ n_s · (6·f_s + 2 + Σ_f len(key_f)) ]  +  V_streams
```

where `n_s` is the frozen record count of stream `s` and `V_streams = Σ_s Σ_{r∈s} Σ_f len(value_f(r))` is the exact total of all identifier-value bytes across the 27 streams (all 27 projections contain only string identifiers; none contains a u64 or a digest). The field-name lengths are frozen constants (e.g. `lane_id`=7, `behavior_case_id`=16, `obligation_id`=13, `case_id`=7, `judge_id`=8, `bundle_id`=9, `evidence_source_id`=18, `artifact_type`=13, `template_id`=11, `gate_id`=7, `path_id`=7). Substituting the frozen per-stream constants and counts gives the exact closed form

```
U_digest = 35,158 + 177·H + 47·M + 39·R_HE + V_streams
```

with `V_streams` the exact measured identifier-value byte total (occurrence count `N_val = 2,161 + 12H + 3M + 2R_HE` over the 27 streams; each occurrence contributes its actual UTF-8 byte length). The derivation of the fixed and variable coefficients is recomputable from the table below; every coefficient was independently re-summed from the frozen projections and key-name lengths. **The V12–V26 corrections do not change any projection field name, sort tuple, or count in this table — the `template_id` value is the record `id`, the `gate_id` value is the record `owner_id`, the case-id strings in the `case_plan` stream are the same 425 real generated ids (identical on the F46/B2″ synthetic expectation variant — V25 item 1; their intern timing in Phase A is a resource-accounting fact, not a stream change — V22 item 4; the global intern-table collision rule of V23 item 2 — hit-reuse with zero charge, miss-only allocation — and the V24/V25/V26 fixture/live-basis scoping change no stream, count, or projection), and the V14–V26 owner assignments, phase reordering, S8/W_B/h_max charge-accounting, counter-class partition, modeled-inventory scoping, charge-accounting clarifications, termination-argument replacement, fixture-construction freeze (including the F46/B2″ synthetic expectation variant of V25 item 1 — the gate id values in the gate-projecting streams are longer on that family, but the F46/B2″ fixtures never reach Phase D/E, and the stream projections/counts are unchanged), live-basis re-verification, and re-verification add no stream — so `U_digest` is unchanged as a formula.**

| Stream | f_s | Σ len(key) | n_s | per-record constant (incl. LF) | n_s·constant |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| lane | 1 | 7 | 12 | 15 | 180 |
| behavior_case | 2 | 23 | 46 | 37 | 1,702 |
| case_plan | 2 | 14 | 425 | 28 | 11,900 |
| obligation | 3 | 36 | 46 | 56 | 2,576 |
| judge | 1 | 8 | 46 | 16 | 736 |
| evidence_bundle | 2 | 16 | 12 | 30 | 360 |
| evidence_source | 1 | 18 | 41 | 26 | 1,066 |
| lane_context_contract | 1 | 13 | 2 | 21 | 42 |
| post_gate_output_contract | 1 | 13 | 2 | 21 | 42 |
| gate | 2 | 14 | 58 | 28 | 1,624 |
| judge_negative_template | 1 | 11 | 46 | 19 | 874 |
| aggregate_negative_template | 1 | 11 | 58 | 19 | 1,102 |
| plumbing_negative_template | 1 | 11 | 58 | 19 | 1,102 |
| enforcement_path | 6 | 51 | H | 89 | 89·H |
| behavior_obligation | 3 | 36 | 46 | 56 | 2,576 |
| obligation_judge | 2 | 21 | 46 | 35 | 1,610 |
| lane_bundle | 2 | 16 | 12 | 30 | 360 |
| obligation_gate | 3 | 27 | M | 47 | 47·M |
| bundle_evidence | 2 | 27 | 52 | 41 | 2,132 |
| lane_context | 2 | 20 | 21 | 34 | 714 |
| lane_output | 2 | 20 | 22 | 34 | 748 |
| path_case | 2 | 14 | H | 28 | 28·H |
| path_gate | 2 | 14 | H | 28 | 28·H |
| path_evidence | 2 | 25 | R_HE | 39 | 39·R_HE |
| path_judge_negative | 2 | 18 | H | 32 | 32·H |
| gate_aggregate_negative | 2 | 18 | 58 | 32 | 1,856 |
| gate_plumbing_negative | 2 | 18 | 58 | 32 | 1,856 |

Fixed constants sum to 35,158; variable coefficients sum to `(89+28+28+32)H + 47M + 39R_HE = 177H + 47M + 39R_HE`. The compiler computes `U_digest` exactly while streaming the 27 preimages (§11); it is a work measure, not a retention measure, and no preimage is retained.

**Value-occurrence decomposition and the corrected finite bound (V8 disposition item 6).** Counting value fields across the 27 streams gives `N_val = 2,161 + 12H + 3M + 2R_HE` identifier-value occurrences (the constant part `2,161` is the sum of the frozen constant-stream value counts; `12H` from `enforcement_path` (6 per H) plus `path_case`/`path_gate`/`path_judge_negative` (2 each per H); `3M` from `obligation_gate`; `2R_HE` from `path_evidence`). The `path_id` value occurs in exactly five streams — `enforcement_path`, `path_case`, `path_gate`, `path_judge_negative` (once per H record each) and `path_evidence` (once per R_HE record) — so `path_id` occurs exactly `4H + R_HE` times. All other identifier values therefore occur exactly

```
N_val − (4H + R_HE) = 2,161 + 8H + 3M + R_HE
```

times. Let `L_id_max` be the maximum admitted identifier byte length. Under the frozen identifier grammar `^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9._-]*$` (§7.1) every identifier byte is in the unreserved set `UR`, so `pct_encode` is the identity on identifiers and **does not expand them**; and the derived enforcement-path composite `h = enc(o)~enc(c)~enc(j)~enc(d)~enc(g)` is five encoded identifiers plus four literal `~` separators, so `L_h_max <= 5·L_id_max + 4`. The correct finite value-byte bound is therefore

```
V_streams <= (2,161 + 8H + 3M + R_HE)·L_id_max + (4H + R_HE)·(5·L_id_max + 4)
```

and substituting after the record-syntax constants gives the finite `U_digest` upper bound

```
U_digest <= 35,158 + 177H + 47M + 39R_HE
           + (2,161 + 8H + 3M + R_HE)·L_id_max
           + (4H + R_HE)·(5·L_id_max + 4)
```

At the upper count endpoint (`H = 8,899`, `M = 225`, `R_HE = 41,883`) this evaluates to `U_digest <= 3,564,209 + 503,306·L_id_max` (machine-checked: `3,254,293 + (2,161+71,192+675+41,883)·L_id_max + 77,479·(5·L_id_max+4) = 3,254,293 + 115,911·L_id_max + 387,395·L_id_max + 309,916 = 3,564,209 + 503,306·L_id_max`). The example expression in the remote V8 review — `(2,161 + 3M + 2R_HE)·L_id_max + (4H + R_HE)·(5·L_id_max + 4)` — omitted the `8H` non-path identifier values and is **not controlling**; its diagnosis that V8's `N_val·L_id_max` bound is invalid remains correct. No unexplained standalone occurrence bound (such as the removed `<=230`) appears anywhere in this design; `U_digest` is carried as its own exact term in the work bound. `L_id_max` is itself bounded by the serialized input caps (`l_max` per input buffer) and the checked arena model.

### Output byte accounting
- `U_key = Σ_{k∈names} (len(k_bytes)+1)` counts each key's UTF-8 bytes plus one LF — the exact `expected_keys.sha256` preimage length.
- The compact JSON `names` array byte count is exactly **`U_key + 2·K_run + 1`**: each of the `K_run` strings contributes `len(k)+2` bytes (two quote bytes per string), the `K_run−1` comma separators contribute `K_run−1` bytes, and the two brackets contribute 2 bytes; the LF bytes counted inside `U_key` are not present in the JSON. Example: keys `"a","bc"` give `U_key = (1+1)+(2+1) = 5` and array `["a","bc"]` = 10 bytes = `5 + 2·2 + 1`, not `5 + 2 + 1 = 8`.
- Therefore **`P = P_nonkey + U_key + 2·K_run + 1`** when `P_nonkey` excludes the names array and includes its field syntax (the `"expected_keys":{"names":` prefix and the closing `],…}` suffix) plus every other plan field — including the frozen constant `repository_control_version` value and the four source-phase digest fields (V14 items 4/5), all exact-counted. `strict_parser_version` is not a plan field and adds nothing to `P_nonkey` (V15-N4 as applied by V16). Both the intermediate expression and the P equation above are the corrected forms; the previous `U_key + K_run + 1` expression is not used anywhere in this design. The conclusions remain valid because the correct value is larger: `K_run ≤ U_key` (each key contributes at least 2 bytes to `U_key`), so the names array is `≤ 3·U_key + 1 = O(U_key)`, and `U_key ≤ P` holds. **Nonempty-key lemma (V8 disposition item 9):** every canonical key in `names` contains at least one ASCII byte — each family key is `family "/" component` with a nonempty family prefix and a nonempty component, and each literal key is a nonempty ASCII word — so each key contributes `len(k)+1 >= 2` bytes to `U_key`; hence `2·K_run <= U_key` and a fortiori `K_run <= U_key`. The new Mathematical Gate is derived from the corrected forms. V28's recursive lexical object-key correction permutes only already-present key/value members; it adds/removes no member and changes no key or value bytes, so `P_nonkey`, `U_key`, `K_run`, the displayed identity, and every p_max boundary remain numerically unchanged (the canonical byte sequence changes, its length does not).
- At `K_run_max`, `p_max/K_run_max = 33,554,432/69,302 = 484.177...` bytes per key before `P_nonkey`; count caps alone do not prove output success. Compiler exact-counts the whole P before publication and the catalog gate must show the real key set and displayed source arrays fit. Oversize yields `resource_output_cap_exceeded` without a partial result.
- **Byte-identical commitment acknowledgment.** The `obligation` entity stream and the `behavior_obligation` relation stream intentionally have byte-identical projections and therefore identical digests (`obligation_sha256 == behavior_obligation_sha256`) under the mandated record shapes; they are two typed commitments over the same canonical bytes, not claimed independent hashes (§2.4). Both are still carried as distinct CoveragePlan fields with distinct meanings (entity-set membership vs relation projection).

### Work bound
- `U_key` is explicit and includes byte comparison and emission cost: the 14 fixed sorted key streams (11 execution-family streams, two attempt-indexed compile-family streams, and one literal stream) are k-way merged; each key participates in O(log 14) comparisons and each comparison visits key bytes, giving `O(U_key·log 14)` byte-comparison visits, plus `U_key` emission bytes (the emission is also part of P). `U_key ≤ P` because the plan embeds the complete sorted names array, whose serialized bytes (`U_key + 2·K_run + 1`) are a sub-part of the plan bytes.
- **Successful compiler work is `O(B + R_total + Q_total + U_digest + U_key·log(14) + P)`.** Justification: parsing visits the `B` admitted input bytes once (the parse pass of §9; the hashing pass at S8 also visits exactly `B` bytes and is a constant-factor duplicate of the parse pass, so it does not change the order); the Phase B semantic sweep streams the path skeleton once (an `O(H + R_HE)` pass that is a sub-part of the `Q_total`/`R_total` validation work and adds no new order term) and evaluates stages 29–40 over the parsed structures; endpoint-reference validation is O(1) per endpoint on interned identifiers (`R_total` endpoints); relation-record validation is O(1) per record (`Q_total` records); the 27 digest preimages are streamed once each, so their serialization visits exactly `U_digest` bytes (the exact total defined above); the key merge and emission cost `O(U_key·log 14)`; output construction visits `P` bytes. The four source-phase commitment preimages (§5.2) are serialized from the already-parsed snapshot records and streamed through the same `h_max` buffer in the Phase A source-commitment subphase (V15-N3 as applied by V16); their bytes are a sub-part of the parsed `B` bytes, so the `B` term covers them and no new term is added (V14 item 5). The S8 hashing pass's one-at-a-time SHA-256 states are a fixed per-buffer constant (256 logical bytes each, released one at a time before the W_B pre-reservation) inside the x_max envelope, and the W_B pre-reservation is a fixed one-time charge (2,712 bytes) inside the x_max envelope; neither adds a new order term (V19 item 1; V20 item 2; V21 items 2 and 4). The single physical `h_max` buffer (65,536 bytes) is the analytic `h_max` constant with two logical reservations at K1 (Phase A) and K4 (Phase D); the Phase D reuse adds no new buffer and no new order term (V22 item 2). The case-id intern substep is O(425) allocation-free lookups plus misses-only allocations, all inside the x_max envelope (V23 item 2, V24 items 2–5). Streaming the 27 digest preimages uses the shared `h_max` buffer one stream at a time; the total `U_digest` byte budget is bounded by the corrected finite bound above, and the work bound's `U_digest` term covers it.

### Extrema and overflow
- Degree extrema: `max D_l = 7 ≤ 16`, `max LC_l = 2`, `max LP_l = 2`, `max O_l·G_l = 28 ≤ 32`, `max C_l·O_l = 380 ≤ 512`, `max C_l·G_l = 560 ≤ 1,024`; every path has exactly one judge-negative mapping and every gate exactly one aggregate and one plumbing mapping (established by stage 40 before any derivation). `H ≤ min(H_max, 425·32) = 8,899 ≤ 32,768`.
- The separable conservative per-lane product bound is `C_l*m_l*D_l<=95*28*7=18,620`; it is intentionally not presented as attainable because maxima occur in different lanes (the exact per-lane maximum under the complete bipartite endpoint is `max_l C_l·O_l·G_l·D_l = 11,200` at lane 6 `residency-and-memory-governor`, `80·4·7·5`; per-lane vector `[720, 27, 1,920, 1,200, 1,440, 2,304, 11,200, 5,040, 6,048, 2,464, 1,920, 7,600]`, sum `41,883 = R_HE_max` — V16-B3 corrected, V17 item B3, V19 re-verified, V20 re-verified, V21 re-verified, V22 re-verified, V23 re-verified, V24 re-verified, V25 re-verified). Exact lane substitution gives the tighter total `R_HE<=41,883`. R, Q, K, and U_digest remain far below u64, but every operation is checked.
- **Arena-bound consequence of the W_B invariant (V17-B1 as applied by V18; V19 item 1; V20 item 2; V21 items 2 and 4):** `x_max/R_total_max = 33,554,432/182,735 = 183.623...` bytes per endpoint before any entity/index/input charge; therefore no unsupported `<=48-byte identifier` assumption is allowed. The compiler computes exact X (peak concurrent charge under the frozen arena schedule of §11, including the W_B pre-reservation at K1/K2 and the case-id intern charge in parse-retained — V22 item 4, V23 item 2, V24 items 2–5) and the catalog-content gate must demonstrate the real frozen bytes satisfy `X<=x_max`. For every Phase-B-admitted input, `parse-retained <= x_max − W_B = 33,551,720`, so the sweep peak `parse-retained + 2,712` is provably `<= x_max`. At the entry preflight, every S8 hash-state retention has `used = 0`, so `0 + 256 <= x_max` and no arena preemption can fire before the parse pass (V19 item 1); the W_B reservation has `used = 0`, so `0 + 2,712 <= x_max` and its logical 46 cannot fire (V20 item 2, V21 item 2); the `h_max` logical reservations fire only if `used + 65,536 > x_max` at K1 (Phase A) or K4 (Phase D), and the Phase D use reuses the single Phase A physical bytearray (V22 item 2); a case-id intern miss reserves only if `used + (16 + len(c)) <= x_max`, and a hit visit reserves nothing (V23 item 2, V24 items 2–5). **F46/B2″ termination (V24 item 1, preserved by V25 item 1): the Phase C charges alone exceed `x_max` — `2,784 + 4,254·8,899 + 24·41,883 = 38,864,322 > 33,554,432` — with zero parse-retained and zero case-id intern charge assumed, so the deterministic stage-46 guard fires at or before the last Phase C reservation for every possible hit/miss sequence; the exact recurrence starts from the actual complete parse-retained charge over the exact fixture bytes and never from a displayed subtotal or lower bound. On the exact F46 fixture of V25 item 1, stages 1–40 pass and Phase C is reached, so the guard fires at the first Phase C reservation whose candidate `used + n` exceeds `x_max`.**
- CoveragePlan contains all K_run names, so output is O(U_key), not constant in H. `P = P_nonkey + U_key + 2·K_run + 1` under the exact serializer, where `P_nonkey` is itself exact-counted and includes every serialized source-file, section, and historical-object display record, the four source-phase digest fields, the frozen version constant `repository_control_version`, and the remaining plan fields. Oversize yields `resource_output_cap_exceeded` without a partial result. Recursive lexical ordering of existing object members changes order but not this exact length or any names-array term; array order and all counts are unchanged.
- One-past and empty boundaries: empty inputs, one-past record/path/name/directory/sort caps, reversed ranges, `end = file_length`, `end = file_length + 1`, zero-length sections, overflow seams, platform-size seams, declared-array checked-sum overflow seams, **the empty non-descriptor path (stage 8, V11 item 4)**, **the accepted multidimensional-memoryview `nbytes` boundary at l_max (V14 item 8 / V15 item B1)**, **the stage-44 first-offender prefix boundary (V15 item B2) and the stage-24/44 timing boundary (V15-N6 as applied by V16)**, **the stage-6 whole-document re-encoding full-counter boundary (V16-N3)**, **the frozen-arena stage-46 boundary at below/equal/above `x_max` with the `8 + 8k` derived-record charges, the Phase B transient-structure charges, and the W_B pre-reservation (V14 item 10 / V15 item B4 / V17 items B2/N2 / V17-B1 as applied by V18 / V19 items 1–2 / V20 item 1 / V21 item 1 / V22 item 1) — including the `x_max − W_B` parse-admission boundary (an input whose parse-retained would land in `(x_max − 2,712, x_max]` fires stage 46 at K1 with the checked candidate `used + n` as computed by the deterministic recurrence, never reaching Phase B — B1″) and the S8 `reserve(256)`-at-`used=0` boundary (always passes; K1-assigned counters — V19 item 1) and the W_B `0 + 2,712` boundary (always passes; K1-assigned counters — V20 item 2, V21 item 2)**, **the K1 three-class `logical_arena_byte_count` partition boundary (a stage-46 parse-time failure asserts the candidate `used + n`; a stage-48 parse-time failure asserts the pre-attempt `used = W_B + parse_so_far`; a class-(iii) K1 failure asserts `2,712 + parse-phase peak`; the S8 and W_B stage-48 values are exactly 0 — V20 item 1, V19-N1; equal numeric values across classes are permitted and do not merge the classes — V21 item 5)**, **the complete stage-48 boundary over the modeled inventory (the allocation-fault seam at each frozen ordinal class with the frozen counter set and pre-attempt `used`; no ordinal in Phase B; no ordinal for the Phase D `h_max` logical reservation — V22 item 2; no ordinal for a case-id intern hit visit — V23 item 2, V24 items 2–5)**, **the case-id substep hit/miss boundary (a hit asserts 0 new arena bytes/no ordinal/no firing point; the next miss asserts the correct next ordinal and K1 position-(f) counters; the C4 fixture asserts 1 hit + 424 misses and the exact case-id substep intern charge 26,559 = 26,618 − (16+43) — V23 item 2, V24 item 4)**, **the F46/B2″ termination boundary (the collision-independent chain `2,784 + 4,254·8,899 + 24·41,883 = 38,864,322 > 33,554,432` with zero parse-retained/zero case-id-intern assumed; no displayed subtotal — V24 item 1; the exact F46 fixture reaches Phase C with stages 1–40 passing and B2″ fires stage 40 at K2 with stage 37 passing — V25 item 1)**, and the fail-stop host boundary (a native `MemoryError` at an excluded allocation propagates unchanged — V21 item 2).

### Sensitivity and design consequences
- Any change to a lane CasePlan/gate/artifact cardinality changes the expectation digest and the ledger declarations; the compiler rejects the drift (`traceability_relation_mismatch`) and the frozen H/R arithmetic no longer authorizes compilation — a new catalog/traceability revision and design review are required.
- Denser obligation-gate mappings increase `H`, `R_HE`, `Q_total`, `R_total`, `U_digest`, and `K_run` monotonically up to the lane-local complete bipartite bounds; the compiler neither invents nor removes pairs.
- Raising any cap is a material design change; lower observed inputs never authorize a runtime increase (accepted rule preserved).
- Design consequences: entity/relation records use digest/count commitments, while the parent-required complete key names remain explicit. Exact X, P, and U_digest are catalog-gate obligations, not assumed from cardinality. Compiler binds input, limit, snapshot, closure, and policy identities so drift invalidates the plan.
- The V12/V13/V14/V15/V16/V17/V18/V19/V20/V21/V22/V23/V24/V25/V26 corrections are driftable-observation corrections, record-shape/ownership (identity-contract and predicate-scope) corrections, predicate-scope/pointer/value-constant/accounting-schedule/boundary-semantics/claim-accuracy/counter-class corrections, the V22 displayed-lower-bound/h_max-physical-count/EnforcementPath-wording/case-id-intern-timing corrections, the V23 subtotal-scoping/recurrence-start and intern-collision-rule precision corrections, the V24 collision-independent-termination/fixture-construction/live-basis-proof/C4-construction/collision-statement-scoping corrections, the V25 synthetic-expectation-variant/prior-intern-wording/reconciliation-count-wording/process-provenance/re-verification corrections, and the V26 occurrence-decomposition/process-provenance/re-verification corrections only; they do not alter any count, byte cap, or bound in this section (§1.2, §16, §22). **The sensitivity ratios in this section are recomputed independently by this candidate from the live expectation bytes and the frozen projections; no reviewer-decoded history is claimed and none is needed (V19 item 4, preserved).** **V27 preserved these results. V28's config-preimage constants, provenance-only argv rule, absolute-string identity grammar, C-contiguous immutable-exporter boundary, sole internal exception, recursive lexical object-key order, and bookkeeping/trust wording are likewise shape/order/predicate precision corrections only; fixed member counts and fixed-size digest representations explain why no count, byte cap, formula, or bound changes.**

---

## 17. Alternatives considered and rejected

1. **Let the compiler re-read source files / run Git.** Rejected: PR 6 scope forbids Git, storage, and custody operations, and the preflight classification requires in-memory interfaces sufficient for a compiler with no Git/filesystem discovery. All repository facts arrive via `AuthoritySnapshot`; PR 7 and PR 5's verifier produce them.
2. **Store only a digest of expected keys.** Rejected: the accepted parent explicitly requires the canonical sorted complete expected key names. V26 stores all names plus count/digest and lets p_max reject pathological identifier expansion.
3. **Rewrite the legacy expectation to lexical object-key order.** Rejected: it mutates accepted artifact bytes, the committed expectation digest, and its authority consumers, which is a field/digest change requiring a separate gate. The compiler therefore enforces semantic canonical form (UTF-8, no BOM, LF, final LF, no duplicate keys, no floats/surrogates, strict types, sorted/unique schema arrays) and binds its exact bytes while grandfathering only its object-key layout. Source reconciliation and the new ledger do satisfy exact two-space lexical re-encoding equality; compact generated/JSONL forms retain their separately specified rules.
4. **Re-implement the full expectation/reconciliation semantic schemas inside the compiler by importing the existing loaders.** Rejected: this stale rationale is removed. The PR 6 private parsers are authoritative over the raw bytes and are the only parsers the compiler uses; the legacy loaders exist solely as cross-check test oracles on accepted bytes and are never imported by the compiler (the compiler cannot import `expectation` because it transitively imports subprocess).
5. **Enumerate enforcement paths explicitly in the ledger.** Rejected: 2,287–8,899 path records would duplicate the deterministic matrix expansion and bloat the ledger; the ledger carries the explicit pair relation and the compiler expands paths deterministically (accepted "does not infer from prose" is preserved because every pair is explicit).
6. **Let `catalog_orphan_source` overlap `authority_missing_source`.** Rejected; they are defined as complementary: missing file record vs. extra snapshot file record cited by no obligation.
7. **Truncate oversize diagnostics.** Rejected: truncation fabricates text; oversize diagnostics are dropped and counted in `discarded_diagnostic_count`.
8. **Treat `CompilerPreconditionViolation` as a ContractFailure variant.** Rejected: the 48 variants are closed; precondition violations are custody/VIOLATION or fail-stop conditions handled by the wrapper, never returned as failure bytes. **This rejection is extended by V14 item 7 to the PermitPayload out-of-u64 byte-count condition: it is a construction-time precondition, never a stage.**
9. **Return a `CompilerResult` wrapper.** Rejected: the exact union return type `CoveragePlan | ContractFailure` is preserved; the value objects expose `canonical_bytes`, `sha256`, and `byte_count`.
10. **Have `compile()` accept the legacy parsed value types or wrapper parameter names.** Rejected: the exact six-argument interface text is literal; the four serialized authority arguments are private nominal envelope types carrying exactly the five `BoundBytesRef` buffers.
11. **Let a private parser reject dispatcher-owned conditions.** Rejected: the parsers retain pair-preserving structured data and structural parse facts; ordering, duplicates, per-lane count drift, empty semantic domains, and negative-template defects are deferred to stages 5/6/24/29–40 so the exact variant algebra stays reachable.
12. **Carry directory identity only in a prose sentence.** Rejected: the stability control record is one exact strict tagged union with `directory_identity` always present (object for present-directory, null otherwise), so the preimage serializes identically under every reading.
13. **Record fabricated absent descendants as stability entries.** Rejected (V7 disposition item 4): a no-follow walk cannot observe a descendant after its first absent path component; stability entries bind only observed objects, and every required absence carries exactly one component-probe witness instead.
14. **Bind `common_dir/worktrees/<name>/gitdir` or a `common_dir/worktrees/gitdir` absence.** Rejected (V7 disposition item 2): the current linked worktree's registration directory is already the resolved git_dir root, so the file is `git_dir/gitdir`; a main worktree has no registration directory to enumerate, and the descriptor never depends on unrelated worktree registrations.
15. **Model the accepted checkout as always a main worktree.** Rejected (V7 disposition item 1): the observed current checkout is a linked worktree; the descriptor carries an explicit `main|linked` `worktree_kind` and treats the observed linked checkout as the real current case.
16. **Retain the unexplained `<=230` digest-occurrence bound.** Rejected (V7 disposition item 7): the work bound carries the exact variable `U_digest` as its own term with the complete frozen derivation from the 27 projections and key-name constants.
17. **Let a direct root-child absence name a kind=`control` record as its parent.** Rejected (V8 disposition item 1): the record at logical key `(N,"")` is the namespace **root** record (kind=`root`, 11 keys), a distinct tagged shape from the kind=`control` present-directory record; witness verification locates the kind=`root` record for `Q=""` and a kind=`control`, `presence=present`, `type=directory` record for nonempty `Q`, so every direct absence is mechanically valid.
18. **Freeze witness/scope keys by rule id, namespace/path tuple, or slash-joined path.** Rejected (V8 disposition item 2): the witness key is deterministically `SHA-256(compact lexical-key JSON + LF of {"namespace":N,"requested_path":P})` and the scope keys are the five literal strings; every other candidate grammar is ambiguous or non-recomputable.
19. **Embed witness records in `control_files`, Class P observations, or absent-root scopes.** Rejected (V8 disposition item 3): `component_probe_witnesses` is the sole store of full witness records; every other occurrence is a reference (`witness_ref`/`scope_ref`) with the exact frozen consumer set per record.
20. **Verify `common_dir/refs` through `control_files`.** Rejected (V8 disposition item 4): `refs` is a present directory and present-directory control records project to nothing in `control_files`; `refs` is verified through its `stability_entries` record plus `recursive_scope_proofs["common-refs"]` with `entry_count >= 1`.
21. **Let Scope W descend into the root `.git` directory in main mode.** Rejected (V8 disposition item 5): the root `.git` name is examined only to cross-check `worktree_git_entry`/`worktree_kind`; Scope W never emits a worktree-namespace record for it and never descends into it, in either worktree mode, so no Git object/control subtree enters Scope W.
22. **Bound `U_digest` by `N_val·L_id_max`.** Rejected (V8 disposition item 6): derived `path_id` composites exceed any single identifier bound; the corrected bound charges non-path identifiers at `L_id_max` and the `4H + R_HE` `path_id` occurrences at `5·L_id_max + 4`.
23. **Claim the V6/V7/V8 author/reviewer persisted sessions are unavailable.** Rejected (V8 disposition item 7): the actual session JSONL records and structured request/header evidence exist and are recorded in §19.
24. **Admit a present `refs/replace` with an `entry_count=0` present-root shape.** Rejected (V9 disposition item 1): a present-root scope always includes its root record, so a present `refs/replace` has `entry_count>=1`; the admitted present-empty shape has `entry_count=1` and the empty-stream digest is reserved for the absent-root shape.
25. **Treat `common-replace-refs` as a mandatory present-directory root.** Rejected (V9 disposition item 4): the mandatory present-directory set is exactly the three namespace roots plus `common_dir/refs`; `common-replace-refs` is a mandatory tagged scope that may be absent-root or present-empty, and scope-root verification is a validator obligation, not a serialized reference edge.
26. **Own repository_control defects at both stages 2/4 and stage 19.** Rejected (V9 disposition item 2): the frozen partition of §3.2 gives every repository_control defect class exactly one owner among stages 1–10 and 19, and no wrong container/scalar type falls through every stage.
27. **Treat SectionRecord identity as path alone.** Rejected (V9 disposition item 3): SectionRecord identity is the full `(path,start,end)` tuple; multiple distinct ranges in one source file are valid, stage 5 owns only `SourceFileRecord` path uniqueness, and stage 17 solely owns duplicate/missing/extra section-range tuples.
28. **Blur serialized reference edges with validator obligations.** Rejected (V9 disposition item 4): edges 1–4 are serialized `witness_ref`/`scope_ref` edges; mandatory scope-root verification is a nonserialized validator obligation; the two classes are never conflated.
29. **Count `ControlFileIdentity` and `StabilityIdentity` as two of the 22 top-level descriptor fields.** Rejected (V9 disposition item 5): they are nested type definitions; the top-level field count is exactly 22 without them.
30. **Treat a scalar outside the u64 domain as a stage-41 checked-arithmetic condition.** Rejected (V9 disposition item 6): out-of-domain and mistyped scalars are grammar failures (stage 2/4 non-descriptor, stage 19 descriptor); stage 41 owns only checked arithmetic overflow over individually admitted u64 operands.
31. **Let stage 10 also own non-string `file_type`/`git_object_type` values.** Rejected (V10 disposition item 1 / V11 item 1): the same non-string value would then satisfy both the stage-2 mistyped-field predicate and the stage-10 predicate, breaking exactly-one-owner. Stage 2 owns every non-string JSON value of either field; stages 9/10 require type-correct strings.
32. **Retain the walk-observed symlinked-component clause in stage 9.** Rejected (V10 disposition item 3 / V11 item 3): no snapshot field carries intermediate-component symlink state and the pure compiler performs no filesystem access, so the clause was unsatisfiable by any admitted input and its pointer had no carrier. Refusal of a walk-observed component symlink is a PR 7 preflight fail-closed fact; stage-9 reachability is carried by the representable `file_type="symlink"`/`git_object_type="symlink"` string values.
33. **Let a type-correct empty non-descriptor path fall through to set equality or downstream referential checks.** Rejected (V11 item 4 / remote V10 review): the empty string is type-correct (passes stage 2), excluded from stage 4, and uncaught by stages 7/8 under their former scopes; it only surfaced at stage 11/25 set equality. Stage 8 now owns it explicitly, before any downstream check, with the exact field pointer.
34. **Own type-correct file-type values at stage 4 as enum grammar.** Rejected (V11 item 1): the snapshot schema's `file_type`/`git_object_type` value semantics are semantic-value checks, not identifier/digest/enum/u64 grammar; stage 4's exclusion of file-type values is total (stage 2 for non-string JSON types, stages 9/10 for type-correct strings), so a string like `"fifo"` is never a stage-4 grammar failure.
35. **Carry `template_id` and `gate_id` as source-record fields on the negative-template arrays.** Rejected (V11 review item 2 / V12 item 2): the three-field shape made record identity internally contradictory — the record carried both `id` and `template_id` while §2.4 asserted template id = record id, leaving the authoritative template identity underdetermined and the aggregate/plumbing rows implying a different convention. V12 restores V10's exact two-field `{owner_id, id}` contract: `owner_id` is the obligation (judge) or gate (aggregate/plumbing) id and `id` is the authoritative template identity everywhere; every projection, relation, bind, and key derives the template id from the record `id` (and the gate id from the record `owner_id`), so no compiler invariant, digest, relation, or count changes.
36. **Record the `common_dir/info/exclude` digest as `8d02c822…`.** Rejected (V11 review item 1 / V12 item 1): that value was copied without recomputation and is false; the live 240-byte file at `/Users/chenyu/Documents/github/TurnVectorBenchmark/.git/info/exclude` hashes to `6671fe83…` (re-verified). V12–V26 record only re-observed values and label every live observation driftable (§19).
37. **Let an exact duplicate `{owner_id,id}` negative-template record reach stage 30.** Rejected (V12 disposition item 2 / remote V12 review Blocker B): the exact duplicate necessarily repeats the template `id` within the array, so the flat stage-5 scalar-ID predicate fires first, and V12's stage-30 negative-template construction was unreachable under the controlling flat reading. V13 freezes the flat reading without implementer choice: stage 5 is the sole owner of every repeated negative-template `id` (same or different `owner_id`, including the exact duplicate record); stage 30 explicitly excludes all three negative-template arrays and remains reachable through a duplicate complete gate entity record and its other listed non-scalar-ID-governed records; stage 6 skips arrays containing a repeated id and owns ordering only for distinct records; stage 40 owns the within-safety-cap defects under the frozen subrule order of row 40 (V16-N1, V17 items B1/N1). This changes no projection, relation, digest, key, count, or bound (§10, §15, §16).
38. **Record the worktree `.gitignore` as 12 bytes.** Rejected (V12 disposition item 1 / remote V12 review Blocker A): live read-only recomputation at `/Users/chenyu/.codex/worktrees/TurnVectorBenchmark-d0-source-contract/.gitignore` gives 42 bytes, sha256 `fbce94a3e23d6b3b51688bac4aff6bfbd2f3515d9d844ec4e06deed9b766c80d`, content `__pycache__/`, `*.py[cod]`, `.venv/`, `.artifacts/` each plus LF; V26 records only re-observed values (§19).
39. **Leave the AuthoritySnapshot required invariants partially owned (V13 R1-B1/R3-B2).** Rejected (V14 item 1): V14 assigns exact owners for every receipt counterexample and every related accepted invariant — `clean_required` cross-authority agreement (stage 13), `SourceFileRecord.no_follow_identity.size == byte_count` (stage 19 nested-type subrule), historical-object set equality both directions (stage 12), catalog header/body id collision (stage 5), body-vs-header `design_gate_revision` (stage 21), predecessor-vs-current-lineage inequality (stage 23), catalog `(lane_id, behavior_case_id)` pair uniqueness (stage 37), `obligation_gate_pairs` lane-local consistency (stage 37), historical-object digest agreement (stage 16) — with the complete required-invariant ownership ledger of §10 proving totality, exclusivity, reachability, and determinism.
40. **Keep the V13 stage-14 predicate (`observed_head != revision` is always a mismatch).** Rejected (V14 item 2 / R2-B1): it rejected every valid verified-descendant snapshot that the accepted source contract and current implementation admit. V14 freezes the relation-aware matrix: exact requires `observed_head == revision` and `ancestry_verified == false`; descendant requires `observed_head != revision` and `ancestry_verified == true`; cross-input comparison uses the pinned revisions only, never `observed_head`.
41. **Keep the V13 stage-35 pointer `/traceability_ledger/entities/gates/<index>/id`.** Rejected (V14 item 3 / R1-B2): gate records are exactly `{lane_id, gate_id}`; the pointer named a nonexistent field. The pointer is `/traceability_ledger/entities/gates/<index>/gate_id`.
42. **Leave `strict_parser_version`/`repository_control_version` and the four source-phase commitments unfrozen.** Rejected (V14 items 4–5 / R1-B3/R3-B3): V14 freezes exact types, grammars, values, sources, equality rules, compatibility behavior, record projections, sort orders, framing, LF rule, empty-stream identities, and standalone/nested relationships (§3.2, §5.2, §10). **V16 adds the plan-field distinction: only `repository_control_version` is a direct CoveragePlan output field; `strict_parser_version` is an input descriptor field committed indirectly through `repository_control_sha256` (V15-N4 as applied by V16).**
43. **Keep the V13 CompilePermit token story (issuer token "created by CompileCustody", separate authorization singleton, entrypoint-token mismatch mapped to a precondition).** Rejected (V14 item 6 / R1-B4): it required an unstated second token and conflicted fail-stop vs precondition semantics. V14 freezes exactly two stated module-level token objects with one disclosure path, issuance-time fail-stop, consumption-time preconditions, a PID fail-stop, and a complete state table (§4).
44. **Assign the PermitPayload out-of-u64 byte-count condition to stage 4 (a serialized-document stage).** Rejected (V14 item 7 / R1-B5): permit construction and consumption precede ContractFailure dispatch, so the assignment was unrepresentable. The condition is representable at the permit-creation boundary (checked-u64 payload validation raising `input_identity_mismatch`) and at the consumption boundary (metadata-only byte-count identity), and is mapped coherently into the accepted VIOLATION model with explicit exclusion from the 48-variant algebra and the diagnostic root (§4, §10).
45. **Account BoundBytesRef bytes with `len(buffer)`.** Rejected (V14 item 8 / R3-B1): a multidimensional read-only C-contiguous format-`'B'` view has `len ==` its first dimension while its actual bytes are `nbytes`; the metadata-first l_max guard and hashing would undercount. V14 uses the actual buffer `nbytes` and freezes dimensionality/format/contiguity/lifetime semantics (§9, §10 stage 45).
46. **Leave the observed counters to a prose "through the failure point" rule.** Rejected (V14 item 9 / R3-B4): traversal strategy could change parsed entity/relation/arena/byte counts for identical failures. V14 freezes the stage-by-stage checkpoint schedule over the frozen single-pass order and pipeline phases (§6, §10). **V16 extends the freeze to the K1 failing positions and the K3 stream-by-stream partial formulas (V15-B1/N1 as applied by V16); V17 extends it to the K2 parsed-only sweep counters and the K3-only-after-stage-40 rule (V17 items B1/B2); V18 extends it to the W_B-inclusive K1/K2 arena values and the Phase A stage-46 enumeration (V17-B1 as applied by V18); V19 extends it to the S8 hash-state counters, the K1 stage-46 value rule, and the K1/K4 stage-48 assignments (V19 items 1–2); V20 extends it to the K1 three-class partition and the complete stage-48 ownership for every enumerated modeled allocation (V20 items 1–2); V21 extends it to the modeled-inventory scope with the fail-stop host boundary, the output-builder preallocation, the Phase B slab-offset access, and the equal-values-across-classes note (V21 items 1–5); V22 extends it to the real case-id basis and exact recurrence, the single physical `h_max` allocation with two logical reservations, the EnforcementPath charge wording, and the 425 derived case-id intern substep (V22 items 1–4); V23 extends it to the exact-intern-subtotal scoping and exact F46 recurrence start and to the global intern-table collision rule for the generated-case-id substep (V23 items 1–2); V24 extends it to the collision-independent F46/B2″ termination chain, the complete collision-free fixture construction, the corrected live-basis proof, the exact C4 collision fixture, and the scoped collision statements (V24 items 1–5); 47. **Leave the arena model partial (unlisted retained structures, charges, lifetimes).** Rejected (V14 item 10 / R3-B5): conforming implementations could retain different maps, sort indices, or views and select different stage-46 outcomes. V14 enumerates every retained structure, unit charge, lifetime, phase, the no-alignment rule, and the high-water interpretation (§11). **V16 aligns the phase labels with the six Phase A–F names and the Phase A source-commitment subphase (V15-N3 as applied by V16); V17 enumerates the Phase B transient structures exactly (V17 item N2); V18 freezes the W_B pre-reservation, its reservation point/lifetime/release, its part-of-X status, and the inner scratch guard, and proves the Phase B stage-46 unreachability invariant (V17-B1 as applied by V18); V19 freezes the S8 hash-state one-at-a-time retention/release accounting with its K1 checkpoint assignment and the K1 stage-48 physical-failure assignment, and freezes the counter-block lifetime (carved at Phase B start, live through stages 29–38, released before stage 39) (V19 items 1 and 6); Phase D/E/F → K4; none in Phase B), and the Phase B no-modeled-allocation invariant (V20 items 2 and 5); V21 scopes the inventory (modeled allocations only, with the fail-stop host boundary), freezes the output-builder single preallocation, freezes the Phase B slab-offset access, and adds the equal-values-across-classes note (V21 items 1–5); V25 freezes the F46/B2″ synthetic expectation variant and its digest ownership (V25 item 1), the exact complete prior-intern-set enumeration rule (V25 item 2), the reconciliation count classes (V25 item 3), and re-verifies every carried claim (V25 item 5); V26 corrects the reconciliation occurrence decomposition (V26 item 1), records the V25 process facts (V26 item 2), and re-verifies every carried claim (V26 item 3).**
48. **Let stage 45 report full parsed authority/catalog/section counters (V14-B1).** Rejected (V15 item B1): stage 45 fires at the metadata-only preflight before any input byte is hashed, decoded, copied, or traversed, so full parsed counts are uncomputable there. V15 freezes the K0 checkpoint: `serialized_input_byte_count = B` and every other observed counter 0, with exact above-cap and overflow definitions (§6, §10 rows 41/45).
49. **Let stage 44 report the full `1+N` catalog count (V14-B2).** Rejected (V15 item B2): stage 44 fires during catalog admission at the first record that crosses a cap, so the full value is uncomputable there. V15 freezes the prefix-through-offender counters with exact header indexing and first-offender semantics (§6, §10 row 44).
50. **Define HistoricalObjectRecord validation as set equality only (V14-B3).** Rejected (V15 item B3): set equality does not own duplicate tuples or duplicate complete records. V15 makes stage 12 a true one-to-one multiset/bijection against the 14 reconciliation mapping sides with missing/extra-tuple-distinct/duplicate-tuple/duplicate-complete subrules, exact first-offender order, pointers, and precedence; the fixed 48-variant order is preserved (§3.1, §10 row 12, §15).
51. **Keep the V14 derived-relation arena charge "8 per endpoint + 8 sequence slot (16 per 2-endpoint record)" (V14-B4).** Rejected (V15 item B4): the formula (`8·2+8 = 24`) contradicts the parenthetical (16), leaves 3-endpoint support relations uncovered, and leaves the container-slot double-charge question open. V15 freezes one exact algebra — one derived-stream container slot plus `k` intern reference slots = `8 + 8k` (24 for k=2, 32 for k=3) — distinguishes parsed-array sequence slots from derived-stream container slots, charges every byte exactly once, covers every 2- and 3-endpoint stream, and lists creation/release phases so the stage-46 first-exceed behavior and `logical_arena_byte_count` are reproducible (§11, §16). **V22 item 3 applies the same exactness to the `EnforcementPath` charge: the fixed part is `40 + 8` (five intern-reference slots plus one derived-stream container slot) and the retained `path_id` composite is charged exactly `len(h)`, giving `48 + len(h)` — no reading may charge the composite at `48 + len(h)` on top of the fixed part.**
52. **Claim that a candidate was any session's only write (V14-B5).** Rejected (V15 item B5): the V14 session's transient deleted write-test contradicts that claim, and the coordinator transport evidence owns session-write/route observations. V15 makes no such claim; the V14 transient write-test is disclosed only as historical process provenance (§19). V16/V17/V18/V19/V20/V21/V22/V23/V24/V25/V26 continue to make no such claim.
53. **Claim the production token "is disclosed only through" the helper (V14-N7).** Rejected (V15 item N7): the module-level token is directly importable by any same-user module, so the disclosure helper and caller-frame checks are misuse-detection/convention, not unbypassable authority. V15 states this exactly while preserving the two-token state machine (§4, §14).
54. **Retain the shadowed stage-40 unbound-template subrule (V14-N3).** Rejected (V15 item N3): an unbound template implies its owner obligation/gate has no pairs, which fires stages 31/35 earlier; the subrule was unreachable as a primary variant. V15 deletes it. **V16 additionally deletes the shadowed standalone R_GNG/R_GNP non-bijection subrule and the standalone semantic multiple-template subrule (V15-B2): each is unreachable as a primary predicate — a non-bijection implies some gate's mapping count ≠ 1 (stage-40 subrule (2)), a repeated template id (stage 5), or an out-of-domain owner (stage 29), and the multiple-template condition makes the owner's mapping count ≥ 2 (stage-40 subrule (2)) or fires stages 31/35 for an owner with no pairs; both are retained only as consequences/diagnostic context under those authoritative owners (§10 row 40, §15, §22).**
55. **Retain the V15 K2 `relation_record_count` formula `315 + M + R_GNG_partial + R_GNP_partial + R_CH_partial + R_GH_partial + R_HNJ_partial + R_HE_partial` (V15-B1).** Rejected (V15-B1 as applied by V16): it double-counts the complete R_GNG/R_GNP counts (58+58) inside the 315 constant and omits the enforcement-path term, so it cannot telescope to `Q_total = 4H + R_HE + M + 315`. V16 freezes the stream-by-stream partial `199 + M + R_GNG_partial + R_GNP_partial + R_CH_partial + R_GH_partial + R_HNJ_partial + R_HE_partial + H_partial` (199 = the six fixed streams without the two gate-template streams) and the endpoint partial `398 + 2M + 2·R_GNG_partial + 2·R_GNP_partial + 11·H_partial + 2·R_HE_partial`, both telescoping exactly to Q_total/R_total with frozen domains, update points, and golden substitutions (§6, §16). **V17 renames this checkpoint K3 (reachable only after stage 40 passes) and corrects golden (c) to `320 + M` (V16-B1, V17 item B1); V19 re-verified it; V20 re-verifies it; V21 re-verifies it; V22 re-verifies it; V23 re-verifies it; V24 re-verifies it; V25 re-verifies it.**
56. **Claim the stage-12 fixed message is unchanged across V13→V14→V15 (V15-B3).** Rejected (V15-B3 as applied by V16): V13's message was `reconciliation mapping has no historical object record`; V14 changed it to `historical object set disagrees with the reconciliation mappings`. V16 states the exact before/after strings and truthful successful/failure byte accounting everywhere (§1.2, §15, §16, §22); V17/V18/V19/V20/V21/V22/V23/V24/V25/V26 preserve this.
57. **Claim a mutation-detection mechanism for admitted immutable buffers (V15-N8).** Rejected (V15-N8 as applied by V16): between-pass mutation is impossible for `bytes` and bytes-backed read-only memoryviews, so no detection mechanism exists or is claimed; the compiler retains the base and performs exactly two read-only traversals (§9, §15).

60. **Retain the V16 K3 golden substitution (c) `321 + M` (V16-B1).** Rejected (V17 item B1): both the frozen formula and the displayed summands sum to `320 + M` (`199 + M + 58 + 58 + 1 + 1 + 1 + 1 + 1`); the `321 + M` value was an internal contradiction in the deterministic-counter contract. V17 freezes `320 + M` and machine-checks every displayed equality (§6, §16); V19 re-verified; V20 re-verifies; V21 re-verifies; V22 re-verifies; V23 re-verifies; V24 re-verifies; V25 re-verifies.

62. **Claim `max_l C_l·O_l·G_l·D_l = 1,600` (V16-B3).** Rejected (V17 item B3): the per-lane product vector is `[720, 27, 1,920, 1,200, 1,440, 2,304, 11,200, 5,040, 6,048, 2,464, 1,920, 7,600]` with maximum `11,200` at lane 6 (`80·4·7·5`); `1,600` equals `C_l·O_l·D_l` for lane 6 with the `G_l = 7` factor dropped. 70. **Read the 56-byte parsed-record charge as a blanket per-record charge for all 3-endpoint-stream source records (V17-N4).** Rejected (V17-N4 as applied by V18): the 56-byte figure applies only to the three-field `obligation_gate_pairs` ledger records; the catalog obligation records (19 fields) that project the `obligation`/`behavior_obligation` streams are charged under the full parsed-object formula (worked example **486 bytes** for the synthetic fixture's first obligation record — V19 item 3, V20 re-verified, V21 re-verified, V22 re-verifies, V23 re-verifies, V24 re-verifies, V25 re-verifies), and the V17 "18 fields" figure is corrected to 19.

74. **Claim a reviewer decoded the author's persisted-session bash history (V18-N1).** Rejected (V19 item 4): the sensitivity ratios and every displayed equality are recomputed independently by this candidate from the live expectation bytes and the frozen stream projections; persisted-session and route observations are coordinator transport evidence owned by the route-evidence supplements; no reviewer-decoded-history claim is made and none is needed — the candidate mathematics stands independently.
75. **State the Phase C first-exceed as "path ≈ 8,507" (V18-N2, as applied by V19 item 5 and corrected by V21 item 1).** Rejected: the marginal lane-6 uniform-charge capacity is `floor(x_max/3,944) = 8,507` (`8,507 × 3,944 = 33,551,608 <= 33,554,432 < 33,555,552 = 8,508 × 3,944`), where `3,944 = 48 + 3,704 + 72 + 120` is the hypothetical uniform per-path charge for a lane-6 path whose five `h` fields each carry a 740-byte identifier (`len(h) = 5·740 + 4 = 3,704`); **this value is retained only as a marginal capacity fact that deliberately excludes all residual live charges (parse-retained and the retained R_GNG/R_GNP 2,784 bytes) and is not the first offender of any claimed concrete input (V21 item 1, preserved under the same restrictions by V22 item 1, V24 item 1, and V25 item 1).** Every claim that the 8,508th path is the exact first exceed, that 8,507 complete paths fit in a concrete admissible input, or that 33,555,552 is an actual `used + n` guard candidate is deleted (V20-B1, resolved by V21 item 1): the actual first-exceed of any concrete fixture is the first reservation in the frozen per-record order (R_GNG/R_GNP first, then per path P, then R_CH/R_GH/R_HNJ, then R_HE; with parse-retained live) whose `used + n` exceeds `x_max`, computed by the deterministic recurrence of V22 item 1 over the actual generated case ids — never a uniform whole-batch product (repair counterexamples B2″ and F46); **the F46/B2″ termination proof no longer displays any parse-retained subtotal or derived lower bound and uses only the collision-independent chain `2,784 + 4,254·8,899 + 24·41,883 = 38,864,322 > 33,554,432` (V24 item 1, preserved by V25 item 1).**

The blanket statement gave one K1 failure class two normative values; V20 freezes the three-class partition (stage 46 `used + n`; stage 48 pre-attempt `used`; every other K1 failure the admitted high-water `2,712 + parse-phase peak`) in every narrative, checkpoint row, diagnostic, test, counterexample, and summary (§1 header, §6, §10, §15, §16, §22); V22 extends the class-(ii) member list to the 425 case-id intern entries (V22 item 4); V23 scopes them to miss entries (V23 item 2); V24 re-verifies (V24 items 2–5); V25 re-verifies (V25 items 1–5); V26 re-verifies (V26 items 1–3).

This makes stage 48 structurally unreachable in Phase B (alongside the W_B-invariant proof that stage 46 is structurally unreachable there), and the invariant is stated in the arena model (§11), the algorithm (§8), row 48 (§10), the stage-48 tests (§15), and the tasks (§21). **V21 item 4 freezes the carve mechanism: the counter block and each one-at-a-time mapping table are fixed numeric-offset regions of the single W_B `bytearray` accessed with allocation-free reads/writes — no dict, map object, memoryview, array wrapper, or per-table buffer is created in Phase B; host interpreter temporaries are excluded under the fail-stop host boundary (V21 item 2).**

81. **Arm the allocation-fault seam in a way that depends on traversal or fails a nondeterministic allocation (V19-N3).** Rejected (V20 item 5): the seam targets exactly one deterministic 1-based modeled-allocation ordinal in the frozen modeled allocation order of §11; it fails exactly that allocation once and then disarms; it is unavailable in production (tests-only helper module, statically unreachable from production modules); and it does not alter traversal (the allocation order, guards, and counters are identical whether or not it is armed). This lets tests exercise every K1/K3/K4 stage-48 branch without changing production behavior. **V21 item 2 scopes the seam's target set to exactly and only the enumerated modeled pipeline allocations; V22 items 2 and 4 add the 425 case-id intern ordinals and the single `h_max` ordinal to the target set and confirm that no ordinal exists for the Phase D `h_max` logical reservation; V23 item 2 scopes the case-id intern ordinals to misses (a hit visit has no ordinal and cannot be targeted); V24 re-verifies (V24 items 2–5); V25 re-verifies (V25 items 1–5); V26 re-verifies (V26 items 1–3).**

## 18. Rollout compatibility with PR 7 and PR 8

- **PR 7** consumes the exact `compile()` signature, `CoveragePlan | ContractFailure` value objects, permit payload and byte counts, the exact token contract (`_PRODUCTION_ENTRY_TOKEN` disclosed to `turnvector_benchmark.compile_custody`; PR 6 ships the path dormant — V14 item 6, misuse-detection wording V15 item N7), snapshot/descriptor schema (including `worktree_kind`, `stability_entries`, `component_probe_witnesses` with the digest witness keys, `recursive_scope_proofs` with the five literal scope keys and the two admitted `common-replace-refs` shapes, `proof_observations` with `witness_ref`/`scope_ref` references, the exact reference graph and consumer sets, size-bearing `StabilityIdentity`, and the frozen `strict_parser_version` const), raw-byte envelopes, and precondition mapping. It performs the qualified walks (including the component-probe absence contract, the root-as-parent rule, the frozen scopes with the unconditional root `.git` exclusion, the separate mandatory file/directory verification paths, and the `refs/replace` state machine — absent-root proof or present-empty root record, with any present entry failing preflight closed before a descriptor), creates the permit after durable START, calls the compiler once, receives complete bytes, revalidates the snapshot/closure, then alone stages and publishes. It cannot inject a storage sink into the compiler or change fields/variants/caps without a new gate. **PR 7 preflight owns the walk-observed component-symlink refusal (V11 item 3):** the PR 7 no-follow walk must fail closed on any symlinked intermediate path component before any admitted descriptor is produced; this fact is not representable in AuthoritySnapshot and has no compiler predicate; PR 7 must also produce `SourceFileRecord.file_type="regular"` and `HistoricalObjectRecord.git_object_type="blob"` for every admitted record (the only values that pass the compiler), so a PR 7 descriptor can never carry a stage-9/10 failure in a qualified run. **The collision-free live basis (V24 item 3) is a current-materialization observation only: PR 7's snapshot and the catalog-content gate's ledger are the future real artifacts, and their identifier domains are covered by the compiler's own disjointness obligations (stages 5/29/37 reject any input-side duplicate or unknown reference; the case-id substep's hit/miss behavior is a pure function of the admitted bytes under the frozen collision rule), so no real-artifact collision can alter any variant, counter, or failure byte.**


**V28 PR 7 integration detail (narrow, no new surface).** PR 7 applies the frozen one-line Git-config decoder, hashes canonical JSON scalars, and emits only the existing three-field config records; it enforces the three `extensions.worktreeConfig` file/proof states. It selects and executes Git command lines and revalidates the recorded `argv` provenance, while the compiler performs structural/canonical/projection checks only. PR 7 captures the three canonical absolute identity strings and matching root copies under no-follow identity controls. Permit issuance uses the one `CompilerInternalError` taxonomy; `issuance_kind` remains internal; and the caller-frame helper remains convention/misuse detection despite same-user direct-token access. These duties add no compiler argument, descriptor/output field, stage, variant, cap, or task.

- **PR 8 / catalog-content gate** authors `authority/traceability-v1.json` canonically per §2 (the schema and private parser ship in PR 6 with synthetic fixtures), the real catalog with the real `binds` digests, and the real obligation-gate pairs; the compiler then validates the frozen `H`, `R_HE`, and all counts against the real bytes. The fixture generator's structure (real expectation join) is the template the gate follows; the gate's negative-template arrays must be the exact two-field `{owner_id, id}` records with `owner_id` the real obligation/gate ids and `id` the real template ids, each `id` distinct within its array, so the frozen stage-5/6/30/40 ownership applies unchanged to the real bytes (with stage 40's frozen subrule order — count drift, then judge mappings in frozen path/owner order, then gate mappings in canonical gate order with per-gate aggregate-then-plumbing interleaving — V16-N1, V17 items B1/N1). The gate must demonstrate the real `X` (under the `8 + 8k` derived-record charge algebra of V15 item B4, the Phase B transient-structure charges of V17 item N2, the W_B pre-reservation of V17-B1 as applied by V18 with the slab implementation of V21 item 4 — i.e., that the real parse-retained charge — including the derived case-id intern entries actually created by the substep's miss visits (V22 item 4; on the real basis, whether all 425 visits miss is a live fact the gate must verify per V24 items 2–3) — satisfies `parse-retained <= x_max − W_B` so the sweep fits and stage 40 always fires at K2 — the S8 hash-state accounting and frozen counter-block lifetime of V19 items 1 and 6, the K1 three-class `logical_arena_byte_count` partition and the complete stage-48 ownership over the modeled inventory of V20 items 1–2 as scoped by V21 item 2 and extended by V22 items 2 and 4, the single physical `h_max` allocation with two logical reservations of V22 item 2, the output-builder single preallocation of V21 item 3, and the allocation-fault seam's deterministic ordinal targeting of V20 item 5), `P`, and `U_digest` values; **the F46/B2″ termination proof is collision-independent (V24 item 1, preserved by V25 item 1): the gate needs no parse-retained subtotal or collision-free premise to prove that the frozen F46/B2″ fixtures breach `x_max` in Phase C — the chain `2,784 + 4,254·8,899 + 24·41,883 = 38,864,322 > 33,554,432` holds for every possible hit/miss sequence, and the exact recurrence (starting from the actual complete parse-retained charge) is executed by the tests; the F46/B2″ fixtures are admissible because their expectation input is the synthetic variant of V25 item 1 (the real gate ids are not reused), and the gate must not read the F46/B2″ fixture bindings as the live expectation binding.**
