## 20. Risks, unknowns, and claim limits

- The ledger's `binds` digests for lane suites, case schemas, judge contracts, bundles, evidence sources, context/output contracts, gates, and negative templates are declared digests the compiler cannot verify without file access; their content correctness is owned by the catalog-content gate and PR 7's closure. The compiler verifies key sets, counts, grammar, and cross-consistency only.
- The exact `m_l` pair assignment and the 9 lanes with `environment` / 10 lanes with `checksums` are frozen by the ledger bytes at the catalog-content gate; the compiler validates shape and bounds, not the semantic justification of each pair. The exact negative-template `{owner_id, id}` records (46 judge / 58 aggregate / 58 plumbing) are likewise frozen by the ledger bytes; the compiler validates the two-field shape, `(owner_id, id)` order, the stage-5 repeated-id/sole-owner rule, the stage-6 distinct-records ordering rule, the stage-30 exclusion of the three arrays, and the stage-40 coverage rules under the frozen subrule order (count drift; judge mapping counts in frozen path/owner order; gate mapping counts in canonical gate order with per-gate aggregate-then-plumbing interleaving — V16-N1, V17 items B1/N1), with template unboundness owned by stages 31/35, wrong owners by stage 29, and the multiple-template condition owned through the mapping-count subrule. **The stage-40 checks run in the Phase B validation-only sweep, so no R_HNJ/R_GNG/R_GNP record and no expanded CasePlan record exists, is hashed, or is counted before stage 40 passes, and a stage-40 defect can never be shadowed by a later derivation-phase resource preemption (V17 item B2; V17-N3 resolved; V17-B1 as applied by V18 proves the Phase B transient reservations cannot preempt; V21 items 2 and 4 prove the sweep performs no modeled physical allocation, so no stage-48 class exists in Phase B; the 425 case-id strings are interned identifiers — on the frozen collision-free basis all 425 visits miss (V23 item 2, V24 items 2–5; the F46/B2″ synthetic expectation variant of V25 item 1 has identical 425 case ids) — never CasePlan records — V22 item 4).**
- The repository-control descriptor models the observed linked worktree as the real current case, but the exact physical identities (devices, inodes, sizes, timestamps, digests) are **driftable** OBSERVED facts, including the root `.gitignore` 42-byte content/digest: PR 7 re-observes and recomputes the complete descriptor — including every stability/observation digest, every component-probe witness, every scope proof (both admitted `common-replace-refs` shapes), and the exact reference graph — immediately before publication and fails closed on any drift. A `refs/replace` state change between observation and publication (absent → present-empty, or present-empty → absent, or any entry appearing) is exactly the drift class PR 7 must fail closed on. **A walk-observed symlinked intermediate path component is a PR 7 preflight fail-closed fact (V11 item 3):** PR 7's no-follow walk rejects it before a descriptor exists; the compiler has no predicate over it because no snapshot field can carry it, and no admitted descriptor can claim it.
- **The verified-descendant ancestry claim (`ancestry_verified=true`) is a caller-supplied snapshot fact from the qualified PR 7 merge-base check; the pure compiler never runs Git and validates the state matrix, not the ancestry itself (V14 item 2).** A false ancestry claim is a PR 7 integrity failure (the wrapper recomputes the descriptor immediately before publication and fails closed on drift), not a compiler-detectable condition.

- **Pure-compiler config/argv limit (V28):** the compiler can establish known config semantics only because the zero/false/true preimages are frozen constants; it does not and cannot recover opaque string preimages from SHA-256. Recorded `argv` is provenance, not execution authorization or proof of command semantics; PR 7 owns command selection, execution, physical-repository comparison, and immediate pre-publication revalidation. These are explicit trust/claim limits, not omitted compiler predicates.

- **The historical-object multiset/bijection (V15 item B3) depends on the exact 14 reconciliation mapping sides; PR 7 must supply the matching snapshot records. The K0–K4 observed-counter schedule (V15 items B1/B2; V16 items B1/N1/N2/N3; V17 items B1/B2/N1/N2/N3; V17 items B1/N1–N4 as applied by V18; V19 items 1–6; V20 items 1–5; V21 items 1–5; V22 items 1–4; V23 items 1–2; V24 items 1–5; V25 items 1–5), the `8 + 8k` derived-record arena algebra (V15 item B4), the exact Phase B transient structures (V17 item N2), the W_B pre-reservation rule (V17-B1 as applied by V18) with its slab implementation (V21 item 4), the S8 hash-state accounting and the K1 stage-46 value rule (V19 items 1–2), the K1 three-class `logical_arena_byte_count` partition, the complete stage-48 ownership over the modeled inventory, the frozen modeled physical-allocation order with the deterministic ordinal fault seam, and the fail-stop host boundary (V20 items 1–2 and 5 as scoped by V21 items 1–2), the output-builder single preallocation (V21 item 3), the real case-id basis and exact recurrence (V22 item 1), the single physical `h_max` allocation with two logical reservations (V22 item 2), the EnforcementPath charge wording (V22 item 3), the 425 derived case-id intern substep (V22 item 4) with the global intern-table collision rule (V23 item 2: the hit/miss sequence is a pure function of the inputs and the frozen prior intern order; the general contract is 425 visits and zero to 425 allocations; V24 items 2–5 freeze the fixture construction, the corrected live-basis proof, the exact C4 fixture, and the scoped collision statements), the F46/B2″ synthetic expectation variant and its digest ownership (V25 item 1: the family binds the synthetic variant's digest `D_exp_syn`, never the live digest; stage-37 gate-set equality holds by construction; the 425 case ids are identical to the live basis), the exact complete prior-intern-set enumeration rule (V25 item 2), the reconciliation count classes (V25 item 3) with the exact occurrence decomposition re-run from the current bytes (V26 item 1: `7 + 17 + 7 + 7 + 16 + 1 + 1 + 1 + 1 + 1 = 59` occurrences, `7 + 7 + 2 + 2 + 2 + 1 + 1 + 1 + 1 + 1 = 25` distinct, uppercase `TurnVector` not counted), and the collision-independent F46/B2″ termination chain and exact recurrence start (V24 item 1, preserved by V25 item 1: `2,784 + 4,254·8,899 + 24·41,883 = 38,864,322 > 33,554,432` assumes zero parse-retained and zero case-id intern charge and is valid for every possible hit/miss sequence; the exact recurrence starts from the actual complete parse-retained charge and never from any displayed subtotal or lower bound) are pure functions of the inputs and the failing stage, so no catalog-content, custody, runtime, performance, D1–D5, or product-readiness claim is made or implied.**
- Precondition violations and fail-stop behavior after START remain PR 7's custody responsibility; PR 6 only defines the exception taxonomy. **The PermitPayload construction-time byte-count precondition (V14 item 7) is likewise a PR 7-visible VIOLATION (`input_identity_mismatch`), never a compiler ContractFailure.**
- The compiler's arena charge is a logical accounting contract with the frozen high-water schedule of §11, not an RSS bound (accepted limitation preserved). The S8 hash states and the W_B pre-reservation are part of that logical contract. **The single physical `h_max` bytearray contributes to `used` only while its logical in-use charge is live (the Phase A subphase and the Phase D reservation windows); while inactive in the private reusable slot it contributes 0, so X is unchanged by the reuse, and M_aux = 37,879,808 is unchanged (V22 item 2).**
- **The one environment-dependent variant (`resource_allocation_failed`) is exercised end-to-end only through the private allocation-fault seam targeting a deterministic 1-based modeled-allocation ordinal in the frozen modeled allocation order (including the S8 hash-state and W_B firing points at K1, the derived case-id intern miss ordinals, and the single `h_max` ordinal — V19 item 1, V20 item 2, V22 items 2 and 4, V23 item 2, V24 items 2–5); a bounded malformed input alone cannot deterministically raise `MemoryError` under the frozen 33,554,432-byte cap at a modeled allocation, and production code cannot install the seam (V19-N3, V20 item 5, V21 item 2). The Phase D `h_max` use is a logical reservation and has no seam ordinal (V22 item 2); a case-id intern hit visit has no ordinal and cannot be targeted (V23 item 2, V24 items 2–5).** **Fail-stop host boundary (V21 item 2, stated as the public limitation): the stage-48 algebra and the seam cover only the enumerated modeled pipeline allocations after their logical reservation succeeds; failure-result materialization (the ContractFailure value object, its message bytes, diagnostic strings, and output bytes) and incidental host-language/runtime object or header allocations are outside that modeled bounded inventory — a native `MemoryError` raised while materializing any ContractFailure result, its diagnostic strings/output bytes, or excluded host bookkeeping propagates unchanged as a fail-stop host failure outside the 48-variant ContractFailure algebra, does not recurse into stage 48, and has no assigned checkpoint/counter. The compiler is therefore not total over all physical allocations of the host; this is by design and is the exact boundary of the stage-48 algebra.**
- **The frozen output-byte determinism depends on the frozen version constants, the frozen commitment preimages, and the frozen checkpoint schedule — including the K1 three-class partition (with equal numeric values across classes permitted — V21 item 5), the complete stage-48 ownership over the modeled inventory, the single physical `h_max` allocation (V22 item 2), the 425 case-id intern substep with its global collision rule (V22 item 4, V23 item 2, V24 items 2–5), and the exact F46 recurrence start (V23 item 1, V24 item 1); all are pure functions of the inputs plus frozen constants, so no catalog-content, custody, runtime, performance, D1–D5, or product-readiness claim is made or implied. The stage-12 fixed message was changed in V14 and is a fixed constant byte of the unchanged 18-field schema (V15-B3 as applied by V16).**
- **Arena-boundary truth (V21 item 1, re-derived by V22 item 1; V24 item 1 deletes every displayed subtotal): the marginal lane-6 uniform-charge capacity `floor(x_max/3,944) = 8,507` deliberately excludes all residual live charges and is not the first offender of any claimed concrete input; the exact first-exceed of any concrete fixture is defined by the deterministic recurrence of §10/§11 over the actual generated case ids, starting from the actual complete parse-retained charge computed from the full frozen charge table over the exact fixture bytes (never from any displayed subtotal or lower bound — V24 item 1), and computed by the tests — no unchecked numeric index is claimed in this design. The B2″/F46 termination proof uses only the collision-independent chain `2,784 + 4,254·8,899 + 24·41,883 = 38,864,322 > 33,554,432` (a lower bound that deliberately assumes zero parse-retained and zero generated-case-id intern charge, valid for every possible hit/miss sequence); no parse-retained subtotal and no derived lower bound is displayed anywhere in this design. The exact 26,618 case-id intern charge and the "all 425 visits are misses" statements are scoped to the frozen collision-free basis/fixtures of V24 item 2 — including the F46/B2″ synthetic expectation variant of V25 item 1, whose 425 case ids are identical to the live basis — and the live basis of V24 item 3.** **The F46/B2″ fixtures' admissibility is a claim limit of this revision: their expectation input is the synthetic variant of V25 item 1 (never the unchanged live expectation bytes, the live digest, or the real gate ids), and their end-to-end reachability (stages 1–40 pass on F46; stage-40-first on B2″ with stage 37 passing) is proven in §10 and §15.**
- Claim limits: PR 6 claims only that the private compiler implements the accepted contract with typed failure algebra, resource accounting, relation/causal validation, and canonical outputs. It claims no production behavior, no adapter readiness, no custody durability, no claim readiness, and no D1–D5 detail.
- **V27 rejection / V28 claim limit:** V27's Mathematical Gate PASS and conforming R3 protocol PASS do not overcome the three DESIGN FAIL receipts; Proposal Revision `7d15158b4b718c5b0e688c1b9d4b3735d45c864633c6a755b3bf310b287feaeb` is permanently rejected with 0/3 PASS. V28 corrects only the indexed design defects and asserts no Advisory, gate, formal-review, implementation, scoreboard, benchmark-evaluation, or product-readiness authorization. V28 remains pre-gate/unaccepted and cannot approve itself.

V25 items 1–5):** exactly six pipeline phases A–F; the S8 hashing pass hashes the five admitted buffers one at a time, each live SHA-256 state retained as 256 logical bytes via `reserve(256)` at `used = 0` and released before the next state and before the W_B pre-reservation, so after the fifth release `used = 0`; the W_B pre-reservation is the **first arena retention inside the six phases A–F after S8** (never "the first retention of the pipeline phases" — the S8 states are entry-preflight retentions outside the six phases, each released before the next and before the W_B reservation), executed after the S8 pass and immediately before the stage-1 parse pass with `used = 0` proved by the explicit release (V19 item 1), retained through the end of Phase B and released immediately before the first Phase C retention; the four source-phase commitments are computed in the Phase A source-commitment subphase; the shared `h_max` buffer's physical allocation is exactly one `bytearray(65,536)` at the subphase start, retained in a private reusable slot through the call, with two logical reservations (K1 subphase, K4 Phase D) and logical releases after each (never physical frees) (V22 item 2); the 425 derived case-id strings are visited in the Phase A substep after all five documents complete structural and stage-6 admission and before stage 7, with the global intern-table collision rule of V23 item 2 as extended by V24 items 2–5 (misses only are interned; the general contract is 425 visits and zero to 425 allocations; on the frozen collision-free basis all 425 visits miss — V22 item 4, V23 item 2, V24 items 2–5; the F46/B2″ synthetic expectation variant of V25 item 1 has identical 425 case ids); Phase B is the validation-only semantic sweep establishing stages 29–40 before any derivation; Phase C is the template-dependent derivation that begins only after every stage 1–40 predicate passes; **the output-builder preallocation executes after the exact stage-47 candidate-total check and immediately before the first Phase E name write (V21 item 3)**; the F46/B2″ termination proof is collision-independent (`2,784 + 4,254·8,899 + 24·41,883 = 38,864,322 > 33,554,432`, V24 item 1).
28. **The exporter-based immutability statement (V15-N8 as corrected by V28):** Python can expose a read-only view over mutable storage, so `readonly` alone is insufficient. Construction follows exporter metadata and admits only exact immutable `bytes`; mutable-exporter views are rejected before payload access. Between-pass mutation is therefore impossible for the admitted family, no detection mechanism exists or is claimed, and the compiler retains references and performs exactly two read-only traversals.
